### Runtime gate state

- GitHub emulator run `32742002688` proved KVM acceleration and full emulator boot; the suite executed `47` tests with `40` passing and `7` failing. The failures are product/test synchronization defects, not an emulator-start failure.
- Manual flow: a fixed `300ms` wait raced route completion after camera-permission fallback; use a bounded frame-by-frame `pumpUntilVisible` for the manual-entry screen and `Add food`.
- CRUD: the Protein editor was obscured by the sticky Save control, so the tap missed and `TextFormField.last` threw. Center it with `Scrollable.ensureVisible(alignment: 0.5)`, then require a hit-testable target and field precondition.
- Goals: after scrolling to Protein and closing the modal, the sliver-header Save key was unmounted. Ensure it is visible again and hit-testable before tapping.
- History/traversal/restart: CI disables animations, `AppMotion.durationOf` becomes zero, and `HistoryScreen` still wraps the changing week/month child in `AnimatedSize`. This raises `RenderAnimatedSize was mutated in its own performLayout`. Under reduced motion render the selected child directly; retain `AnimatedSize` only for normal motion.
- Today empty state: tests still expect `No meals logged yet` / `Tap Scan to photograph your meal`; intentional product copy is `Nothing logged yet` / `Point the camera at your first meal - one tap and it's tracked.` Update only the stale test expectations.
- Runtime evidence upload: `.runtime_evidence/github/` is hidden and `actions/upload-artifact` omitted it because `include-hidden-files` defaults false. Set `include-hidden-files: true` and retain an executable workflow contract test.
- The externally reviewed RED/GREEN plan is accepted: focused failing contracts first, minimal production/workflow fixes, focused tests, complete runtime-evidence contracts, analyzer/full verification, Antigravity post-review, commit/push, then rerun the real GitHub emulator gate and inspect the downloaded evidence artifact.

- **Current task — foreground-notification suppression (2026-08-30).** Branch `fix/scan-photo-flow-viewer`, pushed start `6f73a3a`. Root cause is explicit client behavior, not backend lifecycle ambiguity: FCM `notification` payloads are displayed automatically by the OS only while backgrounded/terminated, while `initNotifications` separately subscribes to `FirebaseMessaging.onMessage` and calls `NotificationService.showForeground`, which creates a local high-priority notification while Calorix is already open. Bounded correction proposal: remove the app-owned local-notification foreground path (`flutter_local_notifications` service field/channel/init/show method, callback, provider init and `onMessage.listen`) while retaining permission/token persistence, background handler, OS-delivered remote payload, `onMessageOpenedApp`, initial-message routing, and existing missing/viewed entry safety. Do not change backend pushes or multi-device token storage in this slice. TDD will add a focused source/behavior contract proving no foreground display path remains and background/opened/initial routing contracts remain wired, then run existing notification routing tests plus focused analysis. Mandatory feature-specific Antigravity pre-review is next before test edits.

That test is replaced with `review settle must not signal ready when candidates go stale mid-settle without revalidation`. The corrected test mounts an outer `ProviderContainer` with a pending `UiDiffCaptureSignal.ready` and `DebugCaptureScreen(targetId: 'review')`, pumps exactly two frames past `pumpWidget` (frame 2 flips `_validated` and mounts the inner fixture container/`ReviewScreen`; frame 3 resolves `reviewEntryProvider` to non-empty candidates, which flips `_CaptureReadinessGate` to `ready` and schedules its two-frame settle without yet finishing it), reads the entry via `ProviderScope.containerOf(tester.element(find.byType(ReviewScreen)))`, saves the first `ReviewCandidate`, then clears that same `FoodEntry.candidates` list in place (no provider notification, so nothing marks the gate dirty). - **Double-quoted FlutterFire parser TDD sequence GREEN at `2026-08-22`**: initial 3/3 intended RED after tightening (happy path RED for missing `DefaultFirebaseOptions`, blank/placeholder RED for field-level defects); intermediate 3 pass + 1 concatenation RED (valid/blank/placeholder double-quoted tests pass; adjacent-expression test intentionally fails); final focused 4/4 pass; full `android_test_apk_contract_test.dart` 56/56 pass in 7m57s; `dart format` 0 changes after format; `bash -n` and `git diff --check` pass. Final behavior: `_dart_field_values` in `tool/ci/prepare_android_test_apk.sh` accepts single-quoted AND double-quoted simple Dart string literals via alternation `(?:'([^'\\$]*)'|"([^"\\$]*)")`, each content class excluding its quote character, backslash, and dollar sign, so interpolation, escape sequences, constructors, and variables are rejected. Adjacent-expression rejection via `(?=\s*[,)])` lookahead after closing quote ensures `apiKey: "valid" + "suffix"` is rejected while `apiKey: "valid"` and `apiKey: "valid",` pass. An empty matched literal is preserved as the empty string; `None`-check distinguishes single from double group. For every `DART_REQUIRED_FIELDS` field, `_dart_field_occurrence_count` counts all textual `field:` assignment occurrences and compares against the safely-parsed literal count from `_dart_field_values`; mismatches produce a generic non-secret error per field. Every textual required-field occurrence must correspond to a safely parsed literal; blanks, placeholders, cross-match, no-secret cleanup behavior are preserved. Values are never printed. Local verification and external review are complete; commit/push/PR/merge still pending.

### Stage 1 local Cuttlefish runtime-evidence gate (2026-08-14)

- Scope: new `tool/runtime_evidence/run_cuttlefish_gate.sh`, the hermetic gate group in `test/tool/runtime_evidence_scripts_test.dart`, `.runtime_evidence/` ignore coverage, plan tracking, and this status file. The user-owned `.mcp.json` remains intentionally dirty and untouched.
- TDD/real-harness RED evidence: the first pinned gate run failed to compile because Dart has no `File.setExecutableSync`; the fixture now uses checked `/bin/chmod`. The next pinned run failed all gate cases because `Platform.pathSeparator` had been used between `PATH` entries; the fixture now uses the POSIX/Windows PATH-list separator. After the hardened review delta, a fresh focused run reached the happy path but failed in `_expectedFingerprint` with `RangeError (end): ... -1`: Dart `\0` had not created the intended NUL fixture delimiter, so the independent oracle split ordinary zero characters. The fixture/oracle now use explicit `\u0000`; hostile paths containing a space, non-ASCII text, a tab, and an embedded newline remain covered.
- Implementation: captures HEAD and a deterministic tracked-worktree fingerprint before build; removes stale canonical/custom APKs; target-builds the e2e matrix into a fresh canonical APK; optionally copies that exact artifact to a custom path; rechecks HEAD/worktree before VM startup; hashes the exact APK; starts/waits through `android-vm`; drives that same path through the standard integration-test driver with `--use-application-binary` and `--no-build`; records SDK/model/`wm size`; captures PNG/logcat; validates PNG signature, first `IHDR`, positive dimensions, and exact `wm size`; re-hashes the APK after drive; writes and independently validates the immutable sidecar; and always calls `android-vm stop` through an `EXIT` trap. There is no separate manual `adb install`.
- Fingerprint hardening: `git ls-files -s -z` feeds a direct pipefail-protected NUL-safe loop; every tracked path is rehashed from worktree bytes with checked `git hash-object`; records retain mode/path, sort under `LC_ALL=C` with `sort -z`, and receive a SHA-256. Enumeration and per-path hash failures are hard failures. Tests cover unstaged build-time source mutation plus paths containing spaces, non-ASCII text, tabs, and newlines.
- Artifact/failure hardening: tests cover happy/default and custom-APK command order, build/e2e/source/HEAD/APK/metadata failures, malformed device facts, stale-APK removal followed by a false-success/no-output build, `git ls-files` and `git hash-object` failures, guaranteed shutdown, and empty/text/truncated/wrong-size screenshot rejection before logcat or metadata.
- Fresh final pinned verification (`ghcr.io/cirruslabs/flutter:3.41.9`, amd64 under ARM qemu): final focused gate group passed **14/14** in 8m51s; the full runtime-evidence file passed **44/44** in 10m37s; focused `fvm flutter analyze test_driver/integration_test.dart test/tool/runtime_evidence_scripts_test.dart --no-pub` returned `No issues found!`; formatter reported `0 changed`; `bash -n tool/runtime_evidence/run_cuttlefish_gate.sh` and `git diff --check` passed. Valid RED had failed because the old sequence logged `android-vm adb install` plus `fvm flutter test` where the contract required one exact-APK `flutter drive`.
- Final Antigravity review: conversation `calorix-runtime-evidence-stage1-20260814`, Gemini 3.6 Flash High, first agreed with one must-fix for the driver's unused import. After the import/format correction and fresh verification, the continued review returned `AGREEMENT_STATUS: agree`, `MUST_FIX: none`, `SHOULD_FIX: none`, `QUESTIONS: none`. Responses contained only the requested review fields/ruling and normal MCP wrapper; `git status` confirmed no reviewer mutation.

- Post-handoff artifact-identity finding: official Flutter integration-test documentation shows `flutter test integration_test/...` builds and installs its own `build/app/outputs/apk/androidTest/...`/test APK. - Implementation: stdlib-only Python CLI with `write` and `validate` subcommands. `write` computes `apkSha256` via `hashlib.sha256`, emits `staleBuildFingerprint: false`, performs a safe atomic write via `tempfile.mkstemp` + `os.replace`, and creates missing parent directories of `--output`. `validate` independently recomputes the APK SHA-256; checks the exact sidecar key set (rejects missing/extra top-level keys); validates `sourceCommitSha` as a 40-hex Git SHA-1, `sourceFingerprint`/`apkSha256` as 64-hex SHA-256, and the UTC ISO 8601 timestamp; rejects empty `sdkVersion`/`deviceModel`; requires `viewportDimensions` to be exactly `{width, height}` with positive integers; rejects stale true and every independent fact mismatch; and rejects a missing APK. ### Task 17 AI-history Stage C physical-capture regression repair (2026-07-29)

- Scope: repair the two defects found by the fresh physical capture in the checkpoint above — the expanding `⌘K` suffix hiding the search icon/hint, and the 120×46 New-chat pill clipping its label to `New`. No other AI History layout, tokens, semantics, or capture infrastructure touched.
- RED: strengthened `test/ai_chat/ai_history_stage_c_parity_test.dart` with 4 rendered-geometry assertions (1 already-passing containment check plus 3 new failing ones). Failures against `b02a58d`: search hint rect width `0.0` (expected `>0`); keyboard badge rect width `778.0` (expected `30..45`, i.e. stretched to the full field width instead of ~34-42px); New-chat label rendered aspect ratio `2.58` vs its natural (unclipped) aspect ratio, difference exceeded the `15%` tolerance (rendered `57×19` vs natural `106×19` — width alone had been clipped while height stayed full, proving truncation rather than scaling). Diagnostic instrumentation (deleted before commit) confirmed root causes: the suffix badge `Container(alignment: Alignment.center, ...)` had no explicit width, so Flutter's `Align`/`Container-with-alignment` sizing rule made it expand to fill the entire bounded suffix width offered by `suffixIconConstraints`, overlapping the prefix icon and hint; the New-chat `Flexible` correctly computed a 58px text budget (120 surface − 20 content padding − 34 disc − 8 gap) but `TextOverflow.clip` cut the label instead of shrinking it to fit.
- GREEN: removed `alignment: Alignment.center` from the search-badge `Container` (`lib/features/ai_chat/ai_history_screen.dart`) so it shrink-wraps to its padded `⌘K` content instead of expanding; wrapped the New-chat `Text('New chat')` in `FittedBox(fit: BoxFit.scaleDown, alignment: Alignment.centerLeft)` inside the existing `Flexible`, preserving the 120×46 surface and 34px gradient disc geometry untouched.
- Test results: `fvm flutter test test/ai_chat/ai_history_stage_c_parity_test.dart --reporter compact` → `32/32` (28 baseline + 4 strengthened/added). Focused command `fvm flutter test test/ai_chat test/debug test/debug_reseed_test.dart --reporter compact` → `240/240`. `fvm flutter analyze` (explicit global binary, `--no-pub`): `No issues found!`. Full `fvm flutter test --reporter compact`: `615` passed plus `1` intentional live-contract skip (net +4 over the prior `611` from the added/strengthened assertions).
- Worker routing: an OpenCode health probe returned no output and timed out after 64 seconds; per the repository's documented fallback path (AGENTS.md §3), Claude Code headless proceeded as the editor for this repair. No opencode edits were made.
- Files changed: `lib/features/ai_chat/ai_history_screen.dart` (production fix), `test/ai_chat/ai_history_stage_c_parity_test.dart` (strengthened RED-first assertions).
- Not yet done: no fresh physical-device capture or visual inspection has been performed against this repair. Do not claim physical-capture or visual-parity validation until a new capture on the explicit device is taken, inspected, and compared against the canonical reference images.

## Primary-source research (Task 3)

| Package | Version | Finding |
|---|---|---|
| `flutter_timezone` | 5.1.0 | `FlutterTimezone.getLocalTimezone()` returns `TimezoneInfo`; the `.identifier` property is the IANA zone string (e.g. `"Europe/Zurich"`). Not a plain String — must access `.identifier`. |
| `timezone` | 0.11.1 | `tz.local` defaults to `Etc/UTC` after `initializeTimeZones()`. Does NOT auto-detect the device timezone. Requires explicit `tz.setLocalLocation(tz.getLocation(identifier))` after fetching the native identifier. `initializeTimeZones()` is synchronous and returns void — it must NOT be awaited. |

Root cause: `uname` reports `x86_64` inside this container and `/proc/cpuinfo` contains no `aarch64`/`arm64` word, only the ARMv8 signature (`CPU architecture: 8` plus `CPU implementer` fields), so the prior `_isArmHost` check missed it and the two tests stayed on the 30s default instead of the intended 90s ARM allowance. Fix (test-only): extended `_isArmHost` in `test/tool/android_test_apk_contract_test.dart` to also match `cpu architecture: 8` together with `cpu implementer`, which stays false on native x86_64 GitHub runners (neither field is present there). Timeout duration, test scope, and assertions unchanged; no workflow/app/`.mcp.json` file touched.
